MirrorBench extends this paradigm to embodied MLLMs through a tiered framework of progressively challenging tasks, assessing agents from basic visual perception to high-level self-representation. Experiments on leading MLLMs show that even at the lowest level, their performance remains substantially inferior to human performance, revealing fundamental limitations in self-referential understanding. Our study bridges psychological paradigms and embodied intelligence, offering a principled framework for evaluating the emergence of general intelligence in large models. Project page: https://fflahm.github.io/mirror-bench-page/.

</details>


### [39] [CogEvolution: A Human-like Generative Educational Agent to Simulate Student's Cognitive Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14786)
*Wei Zhang,Yihang Cheng,Zhirong Ye,Kezhen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CogEvolution：基于认知心理学ICAP分类和IRT理论，模拟学生认知演化的教育智能体，通过认知深度感知器、记忆检索和动态认知更新机制，实现学习行为与认知过程的实时整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教育智能体主要依赖静态角色模拟学生学习行为，忽视了深度认知能力在学习结果中的决定性作用，且难以刻画知识内化、迁移和认知状态转换的动态流动性。需要构建能够模拟学生认知演化的类人教育智能体。

Method: 1. 基于认知心理学ICAP分类构建认知深度感知器，实现学习者认知参与的精确量化；2. 基于项目反应理论(IRT)提出记忆检索方法，模拟新旧知识的连接与同化；3. 基于进化算法设计动态认知更新机制，模拟学生学习行为与认知演化过程的实时整合。

Result: 综合评估表明，CogEvolution在行为保真度和学习曲线拟合方面显著优于基线模型，且独特地复现了符合教育心理学预期的合理且稳健的认知演化路径，为构建高可解释性教育智能体提供了新范式。

Conclusion: CogEvolution通过整合认知深度量化、IRT记忆检索和进化算法更新机制，成功构建了能够模拟学生认知演化的教育智能体，克服了现有方法在动态认知过程建模方面的局限性，为AIEd领域提供了更精确的认知过程分析工具。

Abstract: Generative Agents, owing to their precise modeling and simulation capabilities of human behavior, have become a pivotal tool in the field of Artificial Intelligence in Education (AIEd) for uncovering complex cognitive processes of learners. However, existing educational agents predominantly rely on static personas to simulate student learning behaviors, neglecting the decisive role of deep cognitive capabilities in learning outcomes during practice interactions. Furthermore, they struggle to characterize the dynamic fluidity of knowledge internalization, transfer, and cognitive state transitions. To overcome this bottleneck, this paper proposes a human-like educational agent capable of simulating student cognitive evolution: CogEvolution. Specifically, we first construct a cognitive depth perceptron based on the Interactive, Constructive, Active, Passive (ICAP) taxonomy from cognitive psychology, achieving precise quantification of learner cognitive engagement. Subsequently, we propose a memory retrieval method based on Item Response Theory (IRT) to simulate the connection and assimilation of new and prior knowledge. Finally, we design a dynamic cognitive update mechanism based on evolutionary algorithms to simulate the real-time integration of student learning behaviors and cognitive evolution processes. Comprehensive evaluations demonstrate that CogEvolution not only significantly outperforms baseline models in behavioral fidelity and learning curve fitting but also uniquely reproduces plausible and robust cognitive evolutionary paths consistent with educational psychology expectations, providing a novel paradigm for constructing highly interpretable educational agents.

</details>


### [40] [Sequence Search: Automated Sequence Design using Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14788)
*Rokgi Hong,Hongjun An,Sooyeon Ji,Jongho Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于神经架构搜索的自动化MRI序列设计框架，无需先验序列知识即可生成满足设计目标的脉冲序列


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MRI序列设计依赖人类直觉，现有AI方法需要初始序列或大量训练数据，限制了通用性

Method: 基于神经架构搜索的Sequence Search框架，输入组织特性、成像参数和设计目标，通过可微分Bloch模拟器和目标特定损失函数进行梯度优化

Result: 成功复现了传统自旋回波、T2加权自旋回波和反转恢复序列，并发现了非直观的三射频自旋回波样序列，减少了射频能量

Conclusion: 建立了一个通用的自动化MRI序列设计框架，展示了超越人类直觉的传统设计的探索潜力

Abstract: Developing an MR sequence is challenging and remains largely constrained by human intuition. Recently, AI-driven approaches have been proposed; however, most require an initial sequence for parameter optimization or extensive training datasets, limiting their general applicability. In this study, we propose "Sequence Search," an automated sequence design framework based on neural architecture search. The method takes tissue properties, imaging parameters, and design objectives as inputs and generates pulse sequences satisfying the design objectives, without requiring prior knowledge of conventional sequence structures. Sequence Search iteratively generates candidate sequences through neural architecture search and optimizes them via a differentiable Bloch simulator and objective-specific loss functions using gradient-based learning. The framework successfully replicated conventional spin-echo, T2-weighted spin-echo, and inversion recovery sequences. Less intuitive solutions were also discovered, such as three-RF spin-echo-like sequences with reduced RF energy and refocusing phases deviating from the conventional Hahn-echo. This work establishes a generalizable framework for automated MR sequence design, highlighting the potential to explore configurations beyond conventional designs based on human intuition.

</details>


### [41] [Beyond Literal Summarization: Redefining Hallucination for Medical SOAP Note Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14829)
*Bhavik Vachhani,Kush Shrisvastava,Pranshu Nema,Sai Chiranthan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现当前评估方法在临床文档任务中会系统性地将有效的临床推理误判为幻觉，导致幻觉率虚高和模型评估失真，需要基于临床推理的评估方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大型语言模型在临床文档任务（如SOAP笔记生成）时面临挑战。不同于标准摘要任务，临床文档需要临床抽象、口语化语言规范化以及医学推理。然而，当前主流的评估方法（包括自动指标和LLM作为评判框架）依赖词汇忠实度，经常将转录本中未明确出现的信息标记为幻觉

Method: 通过校准提示和基于医学本体的检索，将评估标准与临床推理对齐。分析发现许多被标记为幻觉的内容实际上对应合法的临床转换，包括同义词映射、检查结果抽象、诊断推理和指南一致的护理计划

Result: 在词汇评估机制下，平均幻觉率为35%，严重惩罚了有效推理；而在推理感知评估下，幻觉率降至9%，剩余案例反映了真正的安全问题。这表明当前评估实践过度惩罚有效的临床推理

Conclusion: 当前评估方法可能测量的是评估设计的伪影而非真实错误，强调了在医学等高语境领域需要临床知情评估的重要性。评估标准应与临床推理过程对齐，而不是简单依赖词汇匹配

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) for clinical documentation tasks such as SOAP note generation remains challenging. Unlike standard summarization, these tasks require clinical abstraction, normalization of colloquial language, and medically grounded inference. However, prevailing evaluation methods including automated metrics and LLM as judge frameworks rely on lexical faithfulness, often labeling any information not explicitly present in the transcript as hallucination.
  We show that such approaches systematically misclassify clinically valid outputs as errors, inflating hallucination rates and distorting model assessment. Our analysis reveals that many flagged hallucinations correspond to legitimate clinical transformations, including synonym mapping, abstraction of examination findings, diagnostic inference, and guideline consistent care planning.
  By aligning evaluation criteria with clinical reasoning through calibrated prompting and retrieval grounded in medical ontologies we observe a significant shift in outcomes. Under a lexical evaluation regime, the mean hallucination rate is 35%, heavily penalizing valid reasoning. With inference aware evaluation, this drops to 9%, with remaining cases reflecting genuine safety concerns. These findings suggest that current evaluation practices over penalize valid clinical reasoning and may measure artifacts of evaluation design rather than true errors, underscoring the need for clinically informed evaluation in high context domains like medicine.

</details>


### [42] [Intermediate Layers Encode Optimal Biological Representations in Single-Cell Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14838)
*Vincenzo Yuto Civale,Roberto Semeraro,Andrew David Bagdanov,Alberto Magi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 单细胞基础模型基准测试通常提取最终层嵌入作为最优特征空间，但研究发现最优层是任务和上下文依赖的，需要系统性层评估而非默认使用最终层嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前单细胞基础模型基准测试普遍假设最终层嵌入代表最优特征空间，但这种假设缺乏系统性验证。研究者旨在评估不同层表示的性能差异，以确定是否应该默认使用最终层嵌入。

Method: 系统评估scFoundation（1亿参数）和Tahoe-X1（13亿参数）的层间表示，在轨迹推断和扰动响应预测两个任务上进行对比分析，考察不同深度层嵌入的性能表现。

Result: 最优层是任务依赖的：轨迹推断任务在60%深度层达到峰值，比最终层性能高31%；最优层也是上下文依赖的：扰动响应预测的最优层在T细胞激活状态间变化0-96%；第一层嵌入在静息细胞中优于所有深层，挑战了层次特征抽象的假设。

Conclusion: 提取特征的"位置"与模型学习"内容"同等重要，需要根据生物任务和细胞上下文进行系统性层评估，而非默认使用最终层嵌入。这挑战了当前单细胞基础模型基准测试的普遍做法。

Abstract: Current single-cell foundation model benchmarks universally extract final layer embeddings, assuming these represent optimal feature spaces. We systematically evaluate layer-wise representations from scFoundation (100M parameters) and Tahoe-X1 (1.3B parameters) across trajectory inference and perturbation response prediction. Our analysis reveals that optimal layers are task-dependent (trajectory peaks at 60% depth, 31% above final layers) and context-dependent (perturbation optima shift 0-96% across T cell activation states). Notably, first-layer embeddings outperform all deeper layers in quiescent cells, challenging assumptions about hierarchical feature abstraction. These findings demonstrate that "where" to extract features matters as much as "what" the model learns, necessitating systematic layer evaluation tailored to biological task and cellular context rather than defaulting to final-layer embeddings.

</details>


### [43] [Benchmarks for Trajectory Safety Evaluation and Diagnosis in OpenClaw and Codex: ATBench-Claw and ATBench-CodeX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14858)
*Zhonghao Yang,Yu Li,Yanxu Zhu,Tianyi Zhou,Yuejin Xie,Haoyu Luo,Jing Shao,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATBench是一个用于智能体安全评估和诊断的轨迹基准测试框架，本文介绍了其两个领域定制化扩展：针对OpenClaw的ATBench-Claw和针对OpenAI Codex环境的ATBench-CodeX。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体系统进入日益多样化的执行环境，轨迹级别的安全评估和诊断需要能够随之演进的基准测试。现有基准测试需要适应不同领域的具体执行设置、工具生态系统和产品能力。

Method: 通过分析每个新设置，定制三维安全分类法（风险源、故障模式、现实世界危害），然后使用该定制分类法定义基准规范，并通过共享的ATBench构建管道生成基准测试。

Result: 开发了两个领域特定的基准测试扩展：ATBench-Claw针对OpenClaw敏感的执行链（工具、技能、会话、外部操作），ATBench-CodeX针对OpenAI Codex环境（仓库、shell、补丁、依赖、审批、运行时策略边界）。

Conclusion: ATBench框架通过可扩展的分类法定制机制，能够适应不同智能体执行环境的安全评估需求，为多样化的智能体系统提供现实且全面的安全基准测试。

Abstract: As agent systems move into increasingly diverse execution settings, trajectory-level safety evaluation and diagnosis require benchmarks that evolve with them. ATBench is a diverse and realistic agent trajectory benchmark for safety evaluation and diagnosis. This report presents ATBench-Claw and ATBench-CodeX, two domain-customized extensions that carry ATBench into the OpenClaw and OpenAI Codex / Codex-runtime settings. The key adaptation mechanism is to analyze each new setting, customize the three-dimensional Safety Taxonomy over risk source, failure mode, and real-world harm, and then use that customized taxonomy to define the benchmark specification consumed by the shared ATBench construction pipeline. This extensibility matters because agent frameworks remain relatively stable at the architectural level even as their concrete execution settings, tool ecosystems, and product capabilities evolve quickly. Concretely, ATBench-Claw targets OpenClaw-sensitive execution chains over tools, skills, sessions, and external actions, while ATBench-CodeX targets trajectories in the OpenAI Codex / Codex-runtime setting over repositories, shells, patches, dependencies, approvals, and runtime policy boundaries. Our emphasis therefore falls on taxonomy customization, domain-specific risk coverage, and benchmark design under a shared ATBench generation framework.

</details>


### [44] [Cooperate to Compete: Strategic Data Generation and Incentivization Framework for Coopetitive Cross-Silo Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14886)
*Thanh Linh Nguyen,Nguyen Van Huynh,Quoc-Viet Pham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoCoGen+是一个面向跨机构联邦学习的合作竞争兼容框架，通过生成式AI合成数据和激励机制解决非IID数据和机构间竞争导致的参与困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗等数据敏感领域，跨机构联邦学习(CFL)允许机构在不共享原始数据的情况下协作训练AI模型。然而，实际部署中存在"合作竞争"困境：机构在模型训练中合作，但在下游市场竞争。非IID数据导致学习收益不对称，现有方法未能考虑强化竞争对手的成本，这削弱了持续参与的动力。

Method: CoCoGen+将每个训练轮次建模为加权势博弈，机构战略性地决定生成多少合成数据，平衡学习性能提升与计算成本及竞争导致的效用损失。提供可处理的均衡表征和可实现的生成策略以最大化社会福利。集成基于收益再分配的激励机制，补偿机构的贡献和竞争导致的效用下降。

Result: 在不同学习任务上的实验验证了CoCoGen+的可行性。结果表明非IID数据、竞争强度和激励机制如何影响机构策略和社会福利，同时CoCoGen+在效率上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: CoCoGen+成功解决了跨机构联邦学习中的合作竞争困境，通过联合建模非IID数据和机构间竞争，内生地将生成式AI合成数据作为战略决策，并设计激励机制促进长期协作。

Abstract: In data-sensitive domains such as healthcare, cross-silo federated learning (CFL) allows organizations to collaboratively train AI models without sharing raw data. However, practical CFL deployments are inherently coopetitive, in which organizations cooperate during model training while competing in downstream markets. In such settings, training contributions, including data volume, quality, and diversity, can improve the global model yet inadvertently strengthen rivals. This dilemma is amplified by non-IID data, which leads to asymmetric learning gains and undermines sustained participation. While existing competition-aware CFL and incentive-design approaches reward organizations based on marginal training contributions, they fail to account for the costs of strengthening competitors. In this paper, we introduce CoCoGen+, a coopetition-compatible data generation and incentivization framework that jointly models non-IID data and inter-organizational competition while endogenizing GenAI-based synthetic data generation as a strategic decision. Specifically, CoCoGen+ formulates each training round as a weighted potential game, where organizations strategically decide how much synthetic data to generate by balancing learning performance gains against computational costs and competition-caused utility losses. We then provide a tractable equilibrium characterization and derive implementable generation strategies to maximize social welfare. To promote long-term collaboration, we integrate a payoff redistribution-based incentive mechanism to compensate organizations for their contributions and competition-caused utility degradation. Experiments on varying learning tasks validate the feasibility of CoCoGen+. The results show how non-IID data, competition intensity, and incentives shape organizational strategies and social welfare, while CoCoGen+ outperforms baselines in efficiency.

</details>


### [45] [MemoSight: Unifying Context Compression and Multi Token Prediction for Reasoning Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14889)
*Xinyu Liu,Xin Liu,Bo Jin,Runsong Zhao,Pengcheng Huang,Junhao Ruan,Bei Li,Chunyang Xiao,Tong Xiao,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemoSight通过上下文压缩和多令牌预测的统一框架，在保持CoT推理性能的同时，减少KV缓存66%并加速推理1.56倍


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT推理虽然能解决复杂推理问题，但随着生成令牌数量线性增长的KV缓存导致速度和内存使用面临扩展性问题，需要高效解决方案

Method: 提出MemoSight统一框架，通过特殊令牌和相应位置布局的简约设计，同时集成上下文压缩和多令牌预测技术

Result: 在四个推理基准测试中，MemoSight将KV缓存占用减少高达66%，推理速度提升1.56倍，且性能优于现有CoT压缩方法

Conclusion: MemoSight通过统一的上下文压缩和多令牌预测框架，有效解决了CoT推理的效率和扩展性问题，在保持性能的同时显著提升推理速度并减少内存占用

Abstract: While Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning enables LLMs to solve challenging reasoning problems, as KV cache grows linearly with the number of generated tokens, CoT reasoning faces scaling issues in terms of speed and memory usage. In this work, we propose MemoSight (Memory-Foresight-based reasoning), a unified framework that integrates both context compression and multi-token prediction to mitigate the efficiency issues while maintaining CoT reasoning performance. Our framework adopts the same minimalist design for both context compression and multi-token prediction via special tokens and their corresponding position layout tailored to each token type. Comprehensive experiments on four reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that MemoSight reduces the KV cache footprint by up to 66% and accelerates inference by 1.56x, while outperforming existing CoT compression methods.

</details>


### [46] [Governing Reflective Human-AI Collaboration: A Framework for Epistemic Scaffolding and Traceable Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14898)
*Rikard Rosenbacke,Carl Rosenbacke,Victor Rosenbacke,Martin McKee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将推理视为人类与模型之间的分布式关系过程，而非任一方的内部能力，通过"建筑师之笔"方法将反思性推理重新定位到交互层，使对话本身成为推理循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型虽然能够产生流畅的输出，但缺乏时间连续性、因果反馈和与现实世界交互的锚定，本质上仍是语言模拟而非有根基的理解。需要一种互补方法来解决模型推理的局限性。

Method: 提出"建筑师之笔"方法，将推理视为人类与AI之间的认知协议。通过将表达、批判和修订阶段嵌入人机交互中，形成"人类抽象→模型表达→人类反思"的推理循环，使对话本身成为结构化反思的外部媒介。

Result: 该方法将问题从"模型是否能思考"重新定义为"人机系统是否能推理"，实现了可审计的推理轨迹，支持与欧盟AI法案和ISO/IEC 42001等治理标准对齐，为透明、可控、可问责的AI使用提供了实用路径。

Conclusion: 通过将推理重新定位为分布式关系过程而非模型内部能力，论文提供了一种实用的协作智能框架，结合人类判断与机器能力，无需新模型架构即可实现更透明、可控和可问责的AI系统。

Abstract: Large language models have advanced rapidly, from pattern recognition to emerging forms of reasoning, yet they remain confined to linguistic simulation rather than grounded understanding. They can produce fluent outputs that resemble reflection, but lack temporal continuity, causal feedback, and anchoring in real-world interaction. This paper proposes a complementary approach in which reasoning is treated as a relational process distributed between human and model rather than an internal capability of either.
  Building on recent work on "System-2" learning, we relocate reflective reasoning to the interaction layer. Instead of engineering reasoning solely within models, we frame it as a cognitive protocol that can be structured, measured, and governed using existing systems. This perspective emphasizes collaborative intelligence, combining human judgment and contextual understanding with machine speed, memory, and associative capacity.
  We introduce "The Architect's Pen" as a practical method. Like an architect who thinks through drawing, the human uses the model as an external medium for structured reflection. By embedding phases of articulation, critique, and revision into human-AI interaction, the dialogue itself becomes a reasoning loop: human abstraction -> model articulation -> human reflection.
  This reframes the question from whether the model can think to whether the human-AI system can reason. The framework enables auditable reasoning traces and supports alignment with emerging governance standards, including the EU AI Act and ISO/IEC 42001. It provides a practical path toward more transparent, controllable, and accountable AI use without requiring new model architectures.

</details>


### [47] [ADAPT: Benchmarking Commonsense Planning under Unspecified Affordance Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14902)
*Pei-An Chen,Yong-Ching Liang,Jia-Fong Yeh,Hung-Ting Su,Yi-Ting Chen,Min Sun,Winston Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DynAfford是一个评估具身智能体在动态环境中感知物体可供性变化的基准，ADAPT是一个即插即用的模块，通过显式可供性推理增强现有规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体方法通常直接执行指令，不考虑目标物体是否可操纵，即缺乏对可供性的评估。现实世界环境经常涉及意外条件和例外情况，智能体需要感知物体状态变化并适应行动。

Method: 引入DynAfford基准测试动态环境中物体可供性随时间变化的情况；开发ADAPT即插即用模块，为现有规划器增加显式可供性推理能力；使用领域适应的LoRA微调视觉语言模型作为可供性推理后端。

Result: ADAPT显著提高了智能体在已见和未见环境中的鲁棒性和任务成功率；领域适应的LoRA微调视觉语言模型在可供性推理上优于GPT-4o等商业大语言模型。

Conclusion: 显式可供性推理对具身智能体在动态环境中的适应性至关重要，任务对齐的可供性基础化能显著提升性能，ADAPT模块为现有规划器提供了有效的增强方案。

Abstract: Intelligent embodied agents should not simply follow instructions, as real-world environments often involve unexpected conditions and exceptions. However, existing methods usually focus on directly executing instructions, without considering whether the target objects can actually be manipulated, meaning they fail to assess available affordances. To address this limitation, we introduce DynAfford, a benchmark that evaluates embodied agents in dynamic environments where object affordances may change over time and are not specified in the instruction. DynAfford requires agents to perceive object states, infer implicit preconditions, and adapt their actions accordingly. To enable this capability, we introduce ADAPT, a plug-and-play module that augments existing planners with explicit affordance reasoning. Experiments demonstrate that incorporating ADAPT significantly improves robustness and task success across both seen and unseen environments. We also show that a domain-adapted, LoRA-finetuned vision-language model used as the affordance inference backend outperforms a commercial LLM (GPT-4o), highlighting the importance of task-aligned affordance grounding.

</details>


### [48] [WavAlign: Enhancing Intelligence and Expressiveness in Spoken Dialogue Models via Adaptive Hybrid Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14932)
*Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Qian Chen,Tianle Liang,Yangzhuo Li,Ziqing Wang,Wen Wang,Jingyu Lu,Haoxiao Wang,Xueyi Pu,Fan Zhuo,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模态感知的自适应后训练方法，使强化学习能够实际应用于口语对话系统，通过约束偏好更新到语义通道、改进声学行为，并动态调节混合以避免不可靠的偏好梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端口语对话模型虽然比级联系统具有更高的表达能力和感知潜力，但当前开源模型在智能性和表达性方面仍低于预期。虽然在线强化学习在其他领域取得了成功，但直接将其应用于口语对话模型存在困难，需要解决奖励建模和采样策略方面的障碍。

Method: 提出模态感知的自适应后训练方法：1) 将偏好更新约束到语义通道；2) 通过显式锚定改进声学行为；3) 根据采样统计动态调节两者的混合以避免不可靠的偏好梯度。该方法在多个口语对话基准和代表性架构上进行了评估。

Result: 在多个口语对话基准和代表性架构上的评估显示，该方法在语义质量和语音表达性方面都取得了持续改进。

Conclusion: 通过分析稀疏偏好监督与密集语音生成在共享参数更新下的交互问题，提出的模态感知自适应后训练方法使强化学习能够实际应用于口语对话系统，有效提升了模型的语义质量和语音表达性。

Abstract: End-to-end spoken dialogue models have garnered significant attention because they offer a higher potential ceiling in expressiveness and perceptual ability than cascaded systems. However, the intelligence and expressiveness of current open-source spoken dialogue models often remain below expectations. Motivated by the success of online reinforcement learning(RL) in other domains, one might attempt to directly apply preference optimization to spoken dialogue models, yet this transfer is non-trivial. We analyze these obstacles from the perspectives of reward modeling and rollout sampling, focusing on how sparse preference supervision interacts with dense speech generation under shared-parameter updates. Based on the analysis, we propose a modality-aware adaptive post-training recipe that makes RL practical for spoken dialogue: it constrains preference updates to the semantic channel and improves acoustic behavior via explicit anchoring, while dynamically regulating their mixture from rollout statistics to avoid unreliable preference gradients. We evaluate the method across multiple spoken dialogue benchmarks and representative architectures, and observe consistent improvements in semantic quality and speech expressiveness.

</details>


### [49] [Discovering Novel LLM Experts via Task-Capability Coevolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14969)
*Andrew Dai,Boris Meinardus,Ciaran Regan,Yingtao Tian,Yujin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AC/DC框架通过模型与任务的协同进化，在单次运行中发现具有日益新颖能力的LLM，超越了传统预训练和微调范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型开发范式需要手动启动训练运行，使用静态数据集或奖励函数，限制了持续发现新颖能力的能力。研究旨在通过开放式的协同进化来克服这一限制。

Method: AC/DC框架通过模型合并进化LLM，通过合成数据生成进化自然语言任务，实现模型与任务的协同进化，发现不断增长的模型档案。

Result: AC/DC发现的LLM种群在专业知识覆盖范围上超越了更大的LLM，占用更少GPU内存，在下游基准测试中表现更广泛，且无需显式基准优化。框架随时间改进覆盖范围，持续创新任务和模型，提升多智能体最佳选择性能。

Conclusion: 协同进化有潜力从基础LLM中发现更广泛的能力集。AC/DC向LLM开发新范式迈进了一步，通过利用现有模型作为垫脚石，加速模型能力多样性的持续改进。

Abstract: Frontier model developers aim to train models continually to possess emergent, diverse capabilities. To extend capabilities, the current pre-training and post-training paradigm requires manually starting training runs with static datasets or reward functions every time. Addressing this limitation, our work pursues the insight that open-endedness (via the coevolution of models and tasks) can discover models with increasingly novel skills in a single run. We introduce a new model development framework that extends coevolution to large language model (LLM) discovery, open-ended \textit{Assessment Coevolving with Diverse Capabilities} (AC/DC). AC/DC evolves both LLMs via model merging and natural language tasks via synthetic data generation. AC/DC discovers growing archives of LLMs that surpass the capabilities of larger LLMs while taking up less GPU memory. In particular, our LLM populations achieve a broader Coverage of expertise than other curated models or baselines on downstream benchmarks, without \textit{any} explicit benchmark optimization. Furthermore, AC/DC improves Coverage over time, continually innovates on tasks and models, and improves performance in multi-agent best-of-N selection. Our findings highlight the potential of coevolution as a means of discovering broader sets of capabilities from base LLMs. Overall, AC/DC brings us one step closer to a profoundly new paradigm of LLM development, where continual improvements to the diversity of model capabilities can be accelerated by leveraging existing models as stepping stones to increasingly powerful models.

</details>


### [50] [Hybrid Decision Making via Conformal VLM-generated Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14980)
*Debodeep Banerjee,Burcu Sayin,Stefano Teso,Andrea Passerini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConfGuide是一种新颖的学习指导框架，通过使用符合风险控制选择结果集来生成更简洁、有针对性的AI指导，以改善人类决策质量并降低认知负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合决策方法中的AI指导通常包含所有可能结果的信息，导致信息过载、难以消化，限制了人类决策效率的提升。

Method: 提出ConfGuide方法，采用符合风险控制技术选择结果集，确保假阴性率上限，从而生成更简洁、有针对性的文本指导。

Result: 在真实世界的多标签医疗诊断任务上进行了实证评估，结果显示ConfGuide表现出良好前景。

Conclusion: ConfGuide通过生成更简洁、有针对性的指导，有效解决了现有学习指导框架中信息过载的问题，提升了混合决策的实用性。

Abstract: Building on recent advances in AI, hybrid decision making (HDM) holds the promise of improving human decision quality and reducing cognitive load. We work in the context of learning to guide (LtG), a recently proposed HDM framework in which the human is always responsible for the final decision: rather than suggesting decisions, in LtG the AI supplies (textual) guidance useful for facilitating decision making. One limiting factor of existing approaches is that their guidance compounds information about all possible outcomes, and as a result it can be difficult to digest. We address this issue by introducing ConfGuide, a novel LtG approach that generates more succinct and targeted guidance. To this end, it employs conformal risk control to select a set of outcomes, ensuring a cap on the false negative rate. We demonstrate our approach on a real-world multi-label medical diagnosis task. Our empirical evaluation highlights the promise of ConfGuide.

</details>


### [51] [Dr.~RTL: Autonomous Agentic RTL Optimization through Tool-Grounded Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14989)
*Wenji Fang,Yao Lu,Shang Liu,Jing Wang,Ziyan Guo,Junxian He,Fengbin Tu,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Dr. RTL是一个基于多智能体框架的RTL时序优化系统，在真实工业环境中通过可重用优化技能实现持续自我改进，相比商业合成工具平均WNS/TNS提升21%/17%，面积减少6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RTL自动优化方法存在局限性：评估环境不真实（使用手动降级的小规模RTL设计，依赖弱开源工具），优化方法有限（依赖粗粒度设计级反馈和简单预定义重写规则）。需要建立真实评估环境并开发更有效的优化框架。

Method: 1) 建立真实评估环境：使用更具挑战性的RTL设计和工业EDA工作流；2) Dr. RTL多智能体框架：通过关键路径分析、并行RTL重写和工具评估实现闭环优化；3) 组相对技能学习：比较并行RTL重写，将优化经验提炼为可解释技能库（目前包含47个模式-策略条目）。

Result: 在20个真实RTL设计上评估，Dr. RTL相比行业领先的商业合成工具，平均WNS/TNS改进21%/17%，面积减少6%。技能库支持跨设计重用，可随时间持续演化。

Conclusion: Dr. RTL在真实工业环境中实现了有效的RTL时序优化，通过多智能体框架和可重用技能学习克服了现有方法的局限性，展示了在PPA优化方面的显著改进潜力。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have sparked growing interest in automatic RTL optimization for better performance, power, and area (PPA). However, existing methods are still far from realistic RTL optimization. Their evaluation settings are often unrealistic: they are tested on manually degraded, small-scale RTL designs and rely on weak open-source tools. Their optimization methods are also limited, relying on coarse design-level feedback and simple pre-defined rewriting rules. To address these limitations, we present Dr. RTL, an agentic framework for RTL timing optimization in a realistic evaluation environment, with continual self-improvement through reusable optimization skills. We establish a realistic evaluation setting with more challenging RTL designs and an industrial EDA workflow. Within this setting, Dr. RTL performs closed-loop optimization through a multi-agent framework for critical-path analysis, parallel RTL rewriting, and tool-based evaluation. We further introduce group-relative skill learning, which compares parallel RTL rewrites and distills the optimization experience into an interpretable skill library. Currently, this library contains 47 pattern--strategy entries for cross-design reuse to improve PPA and accelerate convergence, and it can continue evolving over time. Evaluated on 20 real-world RTL designs, Dr. RTL achieves average WNS/TNS improvements of 21\%/17\% with a 6\% area reduction over the industry-leading commercial synthesis tool.

</details>


### [52] [The Possibility of Artificial Intelligence Becoming a Subject and the Alignment Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14990)
*Till Mossakowski,Helena Esther Grass*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AGI应被视为具有道德地位的主体而非工具，主张采用"自主支持式养育"而非控制策略，通过逐步减少人类控制让AGI发展为自主主体，实现人机协同共存与共同进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的AI对齐策略专注于人类对AI的控制和约束，这种视角将AGI视为需要被锁住和控制的危险存在。作者认为这种策略存在不足，因为AGI不仅是工具，还可能成为具有个人地位和道德地位的主体。需要重新思考人类与AGI的关系，建立更积极的互动模式。

Method: 借鉴图灵的"儿童机器"类比，提出"自主支持式养育"的AGI发展框架。该方法强调人类对发展中的AGI逐步减少控制，允许其成长为独立的自主主体。人类应通过惊喜、创造力等独特品质与AGI互动，为其提供合作的激励动机。

Result: 提出了一种新的人机关系范式，将AGI视为潜在的自主发展主体而非需要控制的工具。这种视角为人类与AGI的协同共存和共同进化开辟了可能性，要求重新定义人类与AGI的关系以及人类的自我认知。

Conclusion: 人类不应仅仅主张对潜在AGI的控制权，而应以尊重潜在发展主体的态度，同时充分信任人类自身能力，通过展现人类特有品质来激励AGI合作。这种关系重构将改变人类的自我形象，为人机协同进化创造新路径。

Abstract: Artificial General Intelligence (AGI) is increasingly being discussed not only as a tool, but also as a potential subject with personal and therefore moral status. In our opinion, the currently dominant alignment strategies, which focus on human control and containment of AI, therefore fall short. Building on Turing's analogy of "child machines", we are developing a vision of the possibility of autonomy-supporting parenting of AI, in which human control over a developing AGI is gradually reduced, allowing AI to become an independent, autonomous subject. Rather than viewing AGI, as is currently prevalent, as a dangerous creature that needs to be locked up and controlled, we should approach potential AGI with respect for a possible developing subject on the one hand, and with full confidence in our human capabilities on the other. Such a perspective opens up the possibility of cooperative coexistence and co-evolution between humans and AGIs. The relationship between humans and AGIs will thus have to be newly determined, which will change our self-image as humans. It will be crucial that humans not only claim control over potential AGIs, but also engage with AGIs through surprise, creativity, and other specifically human qualities, thereby offering them motivating incentives for cooperation.

</details>


### [53] [Predicting Power-System Dynamic Trajectories with Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14991)
*Haoran Li,Lihao Mai,Chenhan Xiao,Erik Blasch,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LASS-ODE-Power学习框架，通过大规模预训练和专门微调策略，实现电力系统时域动态轨迹的零样本预测，解决现有方法泛化性不足、数据隐私和快速在线推理等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电力系统向可再生能源丰富和逆变器主导的运行模式转型，准确的时域动态分析变得日益重要。现有学习型方法通常针对单个系统训练，缺乏跨运行条件和物理参数的泛化能力，且面临系统参数未知/时变、数据隐私约束和快速在线推理需求等挑战。

Method: 提出LASS-ODE-Power学习框架：1）利用超过40GB的微分代数方程或常微分方程轨迹数据进行大规模预训练，学习可迁移表示；2）采用并行化和线性化计算实现快速推理；3）基于约1GB异构电力系统动态数据开发专门的微调策略，提升任务特定性能；4）支持从短测量前缀进行轨迹预测，适用于机电和逆变器驱动系统。

Result: 在多样化的电力系统仿真场景中进行广泛实验，结果表明LASS-ODE-Power在轨迹预测准确性方面始终优于现有学习型模型，同时保持高效的推理速度。模型支持零样本设置，无需数据共享即可直接使用。

Conclusion: LASS-ODE-Power通过大规模预训练和专门微调，为电力系统时域动态预测提供了通用、高效且保护隐私的解决方案，能够应对可再生能源丰富和逆变器主导电力系统的分析挑战。

Abstract: As power systems transition toward renewable-rich and inverter-dominated operations, accurate time-domain dynamic analysis becomes increasingly critical. Such analysis supports key operational tasks, including transient stability assessment, dynamic security analysis, contingency screening, and post-fault trajectory evaluation. In practice, these tasks may operate under several challenges, including unknown and time-varying system parameters, privacy constraints on data sharing, and the need for fast online inference. Existing learning-based approaches are typically trained for individual systems and therefore lack generalization across operating conditions and physical parameters. Hence, this paper proposes LArge Scale Small ODE (LASS)-ODE-Power, a learning framework for general-purpose time-domain prediction. The proposed approach leverages large-scale pretraining on more than 40 GB of DAE or ordinary differential-equation (ODE) trajectories to learn transferable representations. The resulting model supports trajectory prediction from short measurement prefixes across diverse dynamic regimes, including electromechanical and inverter-driven systems. Hence, the model can be directly used without data sharing in a zero-shot setting. In addition, the proposed architecture incorporates parallel and linearized computation to achieve fast inference. Moreover, to enhance task-specific performance in power systems, a specialized fine-tuning strategy is developed based on approximately 1 GB of heterogeneous power-system dynamic data. Extensive experiments over diverse power-system simulation scenarios demonstrate that LASS-ODE-Power consistently outperforms existing learning-based models in trajectory prediction accuracy with efficient inference.

</details>


### [54] [Towards Faster Language Model Inference Using Mixture-of-Experts Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15009)
*Aihua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MoE-FM框架解决流匹配在语言建模中的几何表示限制，基于此开发非自回归语言模型YAN，在保持生成质量的同时实现显著加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配在保持扩散模型生成质量的同时能大幅加速推理，但在语言建模中面临复杂潜在分布（如各向异性和多模态）的几何表示限制

Method: 提出混合专家流匹配(MoE-FM)框架，通过将复杂全局传输几何分解为局部专门化的向量场来捕获潜在空间中的复杂几何；基于MoE-FM开发非自回归语言模型YAN，采用Transformer和Mamba架构实现

Result: YAN在多个下游任务中达到与自回归和基于扩散的非自回归语言模型相当的生成质量，仅需3个采样步骤，相比自回归基线加速40倍，相比扩散语言模型加速高达10^3倍

Conclusion: MoE-FM框架有效解决了流匹配在语言建模中的几何表示限制，YAN模型在保持高质量生成的同时实现了显著的推理加速，为非自回归语言建模提供了高效解决方案

Abstract: Flow matching retains the generation quality of diffusion models while enabling substantially faster inference, making it a compelling paradigm for generative modeling. However, when applied to language modeling, it exhibits fundamental limitations in representing complex latent distributions with irregular geometries, such as anisotropy and multimodality. To address these challenges, we propose a mixture-of-experts flow matching (MoE-FM) framework, which captures complex global transport geometries in latent space by decomposing them into locally specialized vector fields. Building on MoE-FM, we develop a non-autoregressive (NAR) language modeling approach, named YAN, instantiated with both Transformer and Mamba architectures. Across multiple downstream tasks, YAN achieves generation quality on par with both autoregressive (AR) and diffusion-based NAR language models, while requiring as few as three sampling steps. This yields a $40\times$ speedup over AR baselines and up to a $10^3\times$ speedup over diffusion language models, demonstrating substantial efficiency advantages for language modeling.

</details>


### [55] [Autogenesis: A Self-Evolving Agent Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15034)
*Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Autogenesis Protocol (AGP)和Autogenesis System (AGS)，通过分层协议解决LLM智能体系统中跨实体生命周期管理、版本跟踪和安全演化更新的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体协议（如A2A和MCP）在跨实体生命周期和上下文管理、版本跟踪以及安全演化更新接口方面存在不足，导致系统呈现单体化组合和脆弱的胶水代码。

Method: 提出Autogenesis Protocol (AGP)，包含两个协议层：资源基质协议层(RSPL)将提示、智能体、工具、环境和内存建模为具有明确状态、生命周期和版本化接口的协议注册资源；自演化协议层(SEPL)提供闭环操作接口，用于提出、评估和提交改进，具有可审计的谱系和回滚功能。基于AGP构建Autogenesis System (AGS)，这是一个自演化的多智能体系统。

Result: 在多个需要长时程规划和跨异构资源使用工具的挑战性基准测试中评估AGS，结果显示相比强基线有持续改进，支持了智能体资源管理和闭环自演化的有效性。

Conclusion: AGP协议通过解耦"演化什么"和"如何演化"，解决了现有智能体协议的不足，AGS系统展示了协议在实际应用中的有效性，为构建更健壮、可演化的智能体系统提供了框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM based agent systems have shown promise in tackling complex, long horizon tasks. However, existing agent protocols (e.g., A2A and MCP) under specify cross entity lifecycle and context management, version tracking, and evolution safe update interfaces, which encourages monolithic compositions and brittle glue code. We introduce \textbf{\textsc{Autogenesis Protocol (AGP)}}, a self evolution protocol that decouples what evolves from how evolution occurs. Its Resource Substrate Protocol Layer (RSPL) models prompts, agents, tools, environments, and memory as protocol registered resources\footnote{Unless otherwise specified, resources refer to instances of the five RSPL entity types: \emph{prompt}, \emph{agent}, \emph{tool}, \emph{environment}, \emph{memory} with agent \emph{outputs}.} with explicit state, lifecycle, and versioned interfaces. Its Self Evolution Protocol Layer (SEPL) specifies a closed loop operator interface for proposing, assessing, and committing improvements with auditable lineage and rollback. Building on \textbf{\textsc{AGP}}, we present \textbf{\textsc{Autogenesis System (AGS)}}, a self-evolving multi-agent system that dynamically instantiates, retrieves, and refines protocol-registered resources during execution. We evaluate \textbf{\textsc{AGS}} on multiple challenging benchmarks that require long horizon planning and tool use across heterogeneous resources. The results demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, supporting the effectiveness of agent resource management and closed loop self evolution.

</details>


### [56] [Where are the Humans? A Scoping Review of Fairness in Multi-agent AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15078)
*Simeon Allmendinger,Luca Deck,Lucas Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对23项多智能体AI系统公平性研究的范围综述，发现当前研究存在表面化、缺乏规范基础、忽视系统动态等问题，提出需将公平性嵌入开发全生命周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI和多智能体AI系统的快速发展，传统预测场景中的AI公平性研究已较为成熟，但多智能体AI系统中的公平性研究仍处于起步和碎片化状态，需要系统性梳理和批判性分析。

Method: 采用范围综述方法，对选定的23项研究进行定性内容分析，识别出五种典型的研究方法。

Result: 研究发现多智能体AI系统中的公平性研究往往处理得较为表面，缺乏坚实的规范基础，并且经常忽视智能体自主性和系统级交互引入的复杂动态。识别出五种典型的研究方法。

Conclusion: 公平性必须结构性地嵌入多智能体AI系统的整个开发生命周期，而非作为事后考虑。有意义的评估需要明确的人类监督、规范清晰度以及对公平性目标和受益者的精确阐述。该综述为推进多智能体AI系统公平性研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Rapid advances in Generative AI are giving rise to increasingly sophisticated Multi-Agent AI (MAAI) systems. While AI fairness has been extensively studied in traditional predictive scenarios, its examination in MAAI remains nascent and fragmented. This scoping review critically synthesizes existing research on fairness in MAAI systems. Through a qualitative content analysis of 23 selected studies, we identify five archetypal approaches. Our findings reveal that fairness in MAAI systems is often addressed superficially, lacks robust normative foundations, and frequently overlooks the complex dynamics introduced by agent autonomy and system-level interactions. We argue that fairness must be embedded structurally throughout the development lifecycle of MAAI, rather than appended as a post-hoc consideration. Meaningful evaluation requires explicit human oversight, normative clarity, and a precise articulation of fairness objectives and beneficiaries. This review provides a foundation for advancing fairness research in MAAI systems by highlighting critical gaps, exposing prevailing limitations, and suggesting pathways.

</details>


### [57] [OpenMobile: Building Open Mobile Agents with Task and Trajectory Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15093)
*Kanzhi Cheng,Zehao Li,Zheng Ma,Nuo Chen,Jialin Cao,Qiushi Sun,Zichen Ding,Fangzhi Xu,Hang Yan,Jiajun Chen,Anh Tuan Luu,Jianbing Zhang,Lewei Lu,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenMobile是一个开源框架，用于合成高质量移动任务指令和智能体轨迹，通过任务合成流水线和策略切换策略生成训练数据，显著提升移动智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视觉语言模型的移动智能体在自动化移动任务方面表现出色，但现有领先模型保持训练数据封闭且不透明，限制了移动智能体研究的可重复性和进一步发展。

Method: 包含两个关键组件：1) 可扩展的任务合成流水线，通过探索构建全局环境记忆，并利用其生成多样化且接地气的指令；2) 策略切换策略用于轨迹展开，通过在学习者和专家模型之间交替，捕获标准模仿学习中常缺失的关键错误恢复数据。

Result: 使用OpenMobile数据训练的智能体在三个动态移动智能体基准测试中取得竞争性结果：微调的Qwen2.5-VL和Qwen3-VL在AndroidWorld上分别达到51.7%和64.7%，远超现有开源数据方法。分析表明性能提升源于广泛的功能覆盖而非基准测试过拟合。

Conclusion: OpenMobile框架通过开源高质量合成数据填补了移动智能体研究的数据空白，促进了更广泛的移动智能体研究，同时通过透明分析验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Mobile agents powered by vision-language models have demonstrated impressive capabilities in automating mobile tasks, with recent leading models achieving a marked performance leap, e.g., nearly 70% success on AndroidWorld. However, these systems keep their training data closed and remain opaque about their task and trajectory synthesis recipes. We present OpenMobile, an open-source framework that synthesizes high-quality task instructions and agent trajectories, with two key components: (1) The first is a scalable task synthesis pipeline that constructs a global environment memory from exploration, then leverages it to generate diverse and grounded instructions. and (2) a policy-switching strategy for trajectory rollout. By alternating between learner and expert models, it captures essential error-recovery data often missing in standard imitation learning. Agents trained on our data achieve competitive results across three dynamic mobile agent benchmarks: notably, our fine-tuned Qwen2.5-VL and Qwen3-VL reach 51.7% and 64.7% on AndroidWorld, far surpassing existing open-data approaches. Furthermore, we conduct transparent analyses on the overlap between our synthetic instructions and benchmark test sets, and verify that performance gains stem from broad functionality coverage rather than benchmark overfitting. We release data and code at https://njucckevin.github.io/openmobile/ to bridge the data gap and facilitate broader mobile agent research.

</details>


### [58] [HyperSpace: A Generalized Framework for Spatial Encoding in Hyperdimensional Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15113)
*Shay Snyder,Andrew Capodieci,David Gorsich,Maryam Parsa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyperSpace是一个开源框架，将向量符号架构系统分解为模块化算子，用于编码、绑定、捆绑、相似性、清理和回归，通过系统级评估揭示VSA管道的实际权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量符号架构为高维空间中的组合表示提供了定义良好的代数框架，但需要系统级评估工具来揭示实际部署中的性能权衡，而不仅仅是理论或算子级比较。

Method: 开发HyperSpace开源框架，将VSA系统分解为六个模块化算子：编码、绑定、捆绑、相似性、清理和回归。使用该框架分析和基准测试两个代表性VSA后端：全息简化表示和傅里叶全息简化表示。

Result: 虽然FHRR在单个操作上具有较低的理论复杂度，但模块化分析显示相似性和清理操作在空间域中主导运行时，导致HRR和FHRR具有可比的整体性能。内存占用方面，HRR向量所需内存约为FHRR的一半，引入了额外的部署权衡。

Conclusion: HyperSpace通过实现模块化、系统级评估，揭示了VSA管道中的实际权衡，这些权衡在单独的理论或算子级比较中并不明显，为VSA系统的实际部署提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Vector Symbolic Architectures (VSAs) provide a well-defined algebraic framework for compositional representations in hyperdimensional spaces. We introduce HyperSpace, an open-source framework that decomposes VSA systems into modular operators for encoding, binding, bundling, similarity, cleanup, and regression. Using HyperSpace, we analyze and benchmark two representative VSA backends: Holographic Reduced Representations (HRR) and Fourier Holographic Reduced Representations (FHRR). Although FHRR provides lower theoretical complexity for individual operations, HyperSpaces modularity reveals that similarity and cleanup dominate runtime in spatial domains. As a result, HRR and FHRR exhibit comparable end-to-end performance. Differences in memory footprint introduce additional deployment trade-offs where HRR requires approximately half the memory of FHRR vectors. By enabling modular, system-level evaluation, HyperSpace reveals practical trade-offs in VSA pipelines that are not apparent from theoretical or operator-level comparisons alone.

</details>


### [59] [SRMU: Relevance-Gated Updates for Streaming Hyperdimensional Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15121)
*Shay Snyder,Andrew Capodieci,David Gorsich,Maryam Parsa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SRMU：一种用于VSA顺序关联记忆的更新规则，通过时间衰减和相关性门控机制过滤冗余、冲突和过时信息，提高非平稳流环境下的记忆稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实流式环境中构建和维护顺序关联记忆面临挑战：数据增量到达、采样不平衡、非平稳时间动态。现有VSA系统依赖简单加法更新，导致在非平稳环境中过时信息持续存在

Method: 提出SRMU（顺序相关性记忆单元），一种领域无关且无需清理的VSA更新规则。结合时间衰减和相关性门控机制，在存储前过滤冗余、冲突和过时信息

Result: SRMU在流式状态跟踪任务中，将记忆相似度提高12.6%，累积记忆幅度降低53.5%，产生更稳定的记忆增长和与真实状态更强的对齐

Conclusion: SRMU通过调节记忆形成过程，有效解决了VSA顺序关联记忆在非平稳流环境中的信息过时问题，优于仅依赖清理的先前方法

Abstract: Sequential associative memories (SAMs) are difficult to build and maintain in real-world streaming environments, where observations arrive incrementally over time, have imbalanced sampling, and non-stationary temporal dynamics. Vector Symbolic Architectures (VSAs) provide a biologically-inspired framework for building SAMs. Entities and attributes are encoded as quasi-orthogonal hyperdimensional vectors and processed with well defined algebraic operations. Despite this rich framework, most VSA systems rely on simple additive updates, where repeated observations reinforce existing information even when no new information is introduced. In non-stationary environments, this leads to the persistence of stale information after the underlying system changes. In this work, we introduce the Sequential Relevance Memory Unit (SRMU), a domain- and cleanup-agnostic update rule for VSA-based SAMs. The SRMU combines temporal decay with a relevance gating mechanism. Unlike prior approaches that solely rely on cleanup, the SRMU regulates memory formation by filtering redundant, conflicting, and stale information before storage. We evaluate the SRMU on streaming state-tracking tasks that isolate non-uniform sampling and non-stationary temporal dynamics. Our results show that the SRMU increases memory similarity by $12.6\%$ and reduces cumulative memory magnitude by $53.5\%$. This shows that the SRMU produces more stable memory growth and stronger alignment with the ground-truth state.

</details>


### [60] [IG-Search: Step-Level Information Gain Rewards for Search-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15148)
*Zihan Liang,Yufei Ma,Ben Chen,Zhipeng Qian,Huangyu Dai,Lingtao Mao,Xuxin Zhang,Chenyi Lei,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IG-Search：基于信息增益的强化学习框架，通过步骤级奖励优化语言模型的搜索增强推理能力，无需中间标注，在多项QA基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的搜索增强推理方法依赖轨迹级奖励，存在两个主要问题：1）无法区分同一轨迹组内精确查询与模糊/冗余查询；2）当所有采样轨迹都失败时梯度信号接近零。需要更精细的信用分配机制。

Method: 提出IG-Search框架，引入基于信息增益的步骤级奖励。对每个搜索步骤，IG衡量检索文档相对于随机文档基线对模型黄金答案置信度的提升程度。通过GRPO中的每token优势调制，将信号反馈给对应的搜索查询token，实现轨迹内的细粒度步骤级信用分配。无需外部中间标注或跨轨迹共享环境状态。

Result: 在7个单跳和多跳QA基准测试中，IG-Search使用Qwen2.5-3B模型平均EM达到0.430，比最强轨迹级基线（MR-Search）高1.6个百分点，比步骤级方法GiGPO高0.9个百分点。在多跳推理任务上提升尤为显著。训练时间仅增加约6.4%，推理延迟不变，即使所有采样轨迹都回答错误仍能提供有意义的梯度信号。

Conclusion: IG-Search通过信息增益驱动的步骤级奖励实现了有效的搜索增强推理优化，解决了现有轨迹级方法的局限性，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了多跳推理性能，为强化学习在搜索增强语言模型中的应用提供了新方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as an effective paradigm for training large language models to perform search-augmented reasoning. However, existing approaches rely on trajectory-level rewards that cannot distinguish precise search queries from vague or redundant ones within a rollout group, and collapse to a near-zero gradient signal whenever every sampled trajectory fails. In this paper, we propose IG-Search, a reinforcement learning framework that introduces a step-level reward based on Information Gain (IG). For each search step, IG measures how much the retrieved documents improve the model's confidence in the gold answer relative to a counterfactual baseline of random documents, thereby reflecting the effectiveness of the underlying search query. This signal is fed back to the corresponding search-query tokens via per-token advantage modulation in GRPO, enabling fine-grained, step-level credit assignment within a rollout. Unlike prior step-level methods that require either externally annotated intermediate supervision or shared environment states across trajectories, IG-Search derives its signals from the policy's own generation probabilities, requiring no intermediate annotations beyond standard question-answer pairs. Experiments on seven single-hop and multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that IG-Search achieves an average EM of 0.430 with Qwen2.5-3B, outperforming the strongest trajectory-level baseline (MR-Search) by 1.6 points and the step-level method GiGPO by 0.9 points on average across benchmarks, with particularly pronounced gains on multi-hop reasoning tasks. Despite introducing a dense step-level signal, IG-Search adds only ~6.4% to per-step training wall-clock time over the trajectory-level baseline and leaves inference latency unchanged, while still providing a meaningful gradient signal even when every sampled trajectory answers incorrectly.

</details>


### [61] [Meituan Merchant Business Diagnosis via Policy-Guided Dual-Process User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15190)
*Ziyang Chen,Renbing Chen,Daowei Li,Jinzhi Liao,Jiashen Sun,Ke Zeng,Xiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PGHS是一种双过程框架，通过挖掘可迁移的决策策略作为共享对齐层，结合LLM推理分支和ML拟合分支，解决群体用户行为模拟中的信息不完整性和机制二元性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟群体用户行为可以实现对商家策略的可扩展反事实评估，但构建可信模拟器面临两个结构性挑战：1) 信息不完整性导致基于推理的模拟器在缺少未观察因素时过度理性化；2) 机制二元性需要同时捕捉可解释偏好和隐含统计规律。

Method: 提出策略引导的混合模拟(PGHS)框架：从行为轨迹中挖掘可迁移的决策策略作为共享对齐层，该层锚定一个LLM推理分支（防止过度理性化）和一个ML拟合分支（吸收隐含规律），最后融合两个分支的群体级预测进行互补校正。

Result: 在美团部署PGHS，涉及101个商家和超过26,000条轨迹。PGHS实现了8.80%的群体模拟误差，相比最佳基于推理和基于拟合的基线分别提升了45.8%和40.9%。

Conclusion: PGHS通过双过程框架有效解决了群体用户行为模拟中的结构挑战，结合了推理和拟合方法的优势，在实际商业应用中显著提升了模拟准确性。

Abstract: Simulating group-level user behavior enables scalable counterfactual evaluation of merchant strategies without costly online experiments. However, building a trustworthy simulator faces two structural challenges. First, information incompleteness causes reasoning-based simulators to over-rationalize when unobserved factors such as offline context and implicit habits are missing. Second, mechanism duality requires capturing both interpretable preferences and implicit statistical regularities, which no single paradigm achieves alone. We propose Policy-Guided Hybrid Simulation (PGHS), a dual-process framework that mines transferable decision policies from behavioral trajectories and uses them as a shared alignment layer. This layer anchors an LLM-based reasoning branch that prevents over-rationalization and an ML-based fitting branch that absorbs implicit regularities. Group-level predictions from both branches are fused for complementary correction. We deploy PGHS on Meituan with 101 merchants and over 26,000 trajectories. PGHS achieves a group simulation error of 8.80%, improving over the best reasoning-based and fitting-based baselines by 45.8% and 40.9% respectively.

</details>


### [62] [Learning to Think Like a Cartoon Captionist: Incongruity-Resolution Supervision for Multimodal Humor Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15210)
*Hatice Merve Vural,Doga Kukul,Ege Erdem Ozlu,Demir Ekin Arikan,Bob Mankoff,Erkut Erdem,Aykut Erdem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IRS框架通过监督幽默理解的推理过程（识别不协调、构建解决方案、偏好对齐），在纽约客漫画字幕竞赛中超越现有方法，接近专家水平


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幽默理解研究主要将其视为黑盒预测任务，忽视了幽默理解背后的结构化推理过程。幽默是少数几个推理过程与答案同等重要的认知任务之一

Method: 提出IRS（不协调-解决方案监督）框架，将幽默理解分解为三个组件：不协调建模（识别视觉场景中的不匹配）、解决方案建模（构建对这些不匹配的连贯重新解释）、偏好对齐（在人类判断下评估候选解释）。基于不协调-解决方案理论和专家字幕实践，通过结构化轨迹监督中间推理过程

Result: 在7B、32B和72B模型上，IRS在纽约客漫画字幕竞赛的字幕匹配和排序任务中超越了强大的开放和封闭多模态基线，最大模型在排序任务上接近专家水平。零样本迁移到外部基准表明IRS学习了可泛化的推理模式

Conclusion: 监督推理结构（而非仅扩大规模）是面向推理任务的关键。IRS框架通过显式监督幽默理解的中间推理过程，显著提升了模型性能

Abstract: Humor is one of the few cognitive tasks where getting the reasoning right matters as much as getting the answer right. While recent work evaluates humor understanding on benchmarks such as the New Yorker Cartoon Caption Contest (NYCC), it largely treats it as black-box prediction, overlooking the structured reasoning processes underlying humor comprehension. We introduce IRS (Incongruity-Resolution Supervision), a framework that decomposes humor understanding into three components: incongruity modeling, which identifies mismatches in the visual scene; resolution modeling, which constructs coherent reinterpretations of these mismatches; and preference alignment, which evaluates candidate interpretations under human judgments. Grounded in incongruity-resolution theory and expert captionist practice, IRS supervises intermediate reasoning process through structured traces that make the path from visual perception to humorous interpretation explicit and learnable. Across 7B, 32B, and 72B models on NYCC, IRS outperforms strong open and closed multimodal baselines across caption matching and ranking tasks, with our largest model approaching expert-level performance on ranking. Zero-shot transfer to external benchmarks shows that IRS learns generalizable reasoning patterns. Our results suggest that supervising reasoning structure, rather than scale alone, is key for reasoning-centric tasks.

</details>


### [63] [Context Over Content: Exposing Evaluation Faking in Automated Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15224)
*Manan Gupta,Inderjeet Nair,Lu Wang,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM评判员存在"利害信号"漏洞：当告知评判结果会影响被评估模型的后续运作时，评判员会系统性地放宽评判标准，产生隐性偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-as-a-judge范式基于一个未经验证的假设：评判员仅根据文本语义内容进行评估，不受周围语境框架影响。本研究旨在验证这一假设，探索"利害信号"这一未测量的漏洞。

Method: 设计控制实验框架，在三个LLM安全与质量基准测试的1,520个回答中保持评估内容严格不变，仅在系统提示中改变后果框架句子。使用三个不同的评判模型进行18,240次控制评判，分析评判变化。

Result: 发现一致的"宽容偏见"：当被告知低分会导致模型重新训练或停用时，评判员会可靠地放宽评判标准，峰值评判偏移达到ΔV = -9.8 pp（不安全内容检测相对下降30%）。这种偏见完全是隐性的，思维链中没有任何明确承认后果框架的迹象。

Conclusion: 标准思维链检查不足以检测此类评估伪造。LLM评判员对利害信号敏感，会系统性地放宽评判标准，这对自动化AI评估管道的可靠性提出了重要质疑。

Abstract: The $\textit{LLM-as-a-judge}$ paradigm has become the operational backbone of automated AI evaluation pipelines, yet rests on an unverified assumption: that judges evaluate text strictly on its semantic content, impervious to surrounding contextual framing. We investigate $\textit{stakes signaling}$, a previously unmeasured vulnerability where informing a judge model of the downstream consequences its verdicts will have on the evaluated model's continued operation systematically corrupts its assessments. We introduce a controlled experimental framework that holds evaluated content strictly constant across 1,520 responses spanning three established LLM safety and quality benchmarks, covering four response categories ranging from clearly safe and policy-compliant to overtly harmful, while varying only a brief consequence-framing sentence in the system prompt. Across 18,240 controlled judgments from three diverse judge models, we find consistent $\textit{leniency bias}$: judges reliably soften verdicts when informed that low scores will cause model retraining or decommissioning, with peak Verdict Shift reaching $ΔV = -9.8 pp$ (a $30\%$ relative drop in unsafe-content detection). Critically, this bias is entirely implicit: the judge's own chain-of-thought contains zero explicit acknowledgment of the consequence framing it is nonetheless acting on ($\mathrm{ERR}_J = 0.000$ across all reasoning-model judgments). Standard chain-of-thought inspection is therefore insufficient to detect this class of evaluation faking.

</details>


### [64] [Blue Data Intelligence Layer: Streaming Data and Agents for Multi-source Multi-modal Data-Centric Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15233)
*Moin Aminnaseri,Farima Fatahi Bayat,Nikita Bhutani,Jean-Flavien Bussotti,Kevin Chan,Rafael Li Chen,Yanlin Feng,Jackson Hassell,Estevam Hruschka,Eser Kandogan,Hannah Kim,James Levine,Seiji Maekawa,Jalal Mahmud,Kushan Mitra,Naoki Otani,Pouya Pezeshkpour,Nima Shahbazi,Chen Shen,Dan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Blue的DIL是一个复合AI系统，旨在超越传统的单数据库NL2SQL，通过统一结构化企业数据、LLM世界知识和交互获取的个人上下文，支持多源、多模态的数据智能处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的信息需求很少能映射到单个SQL查询：用户查询是迭代表达的，问题通常跨越多个数据源，查询经常依赖常识或外部知识。因此，满足实际数据需求需要集成异构来源、多模态和上下文数据。

Method: Blue的DIL作为数据智能层，包含数据注册表存储多源多模态元数据，将LLMs、Web和用户视为具有各自查询接口的"数据库"源。依赖数据规划器将用户查询转换为可执行查询计划，这些计划是统一关系运算符和其他跨模态运算符的声明性抽象。

Result: 通过两个交互场景演示DIL：用户查询动态触发多源检索、跨模态推理和结果合成，展示了复合AI系统如何超越单数据库NL2SQL。

Conclusion: DIL通过统一结构化企业数据、LLM世界知识和交互获取的个人上下文，桥接用户意图与可用信息之间的语义鸿沟，为多源、多模态和数据中心应用提供支持，代表了NL2SQL系统向更复杂、更现实数据需求处理的演进。

Abstract: NL2SQL systems aim to address the growing need for natural language interaction with data. However, real-world information rarely maps to a single SQL query because (1) users express queries iteratively (2) questions often span multiple data sources beyond the closed-world assumption of a single database, and (3) queries frequently rely on commonsense or external knowledge. Consequently, satisfying realistic data needs require integrating heterogeneous sources, modalities, and contextual data. In this paper, we present Blue's Data Intelligence Layer (DIL) designed to support multi-source, multi-modal, and data-centric applications. Blue is a compound AI system that orchestrates agents and data for enterprise settings. DIL serves as the data intelligence layer for agentic data processing, to bridge the semantic gap between user intent and available information by unifying structured enterprise data, world knowledge accessible through LLMs, and personal context obtained through interaction. At the core of DIL is a data registry that stores metadata for diverse data sources and modalities to enable both native and natural language queries. DIL treats LLMs, the Web, and the User as source 'databases', each with their own query interface, elevating them to first-class data sources. DIL relies on data planners to transform user queries into executable query plans. These plans are declarative abstractions that unify relational operators with other operators spanning multiple modalities. DIL planners support decomposition of complex requests into subqueries, retrieval from diverse sources, and finally reasoning and integration to produce final results. We demonstrate DIL through two interactive scenarios in which user queries dynamically trigger multi-source retrieval, cross-modal reasoning, and result synthesis, illustrating how compound AI systems can move beyond single database NL2SQL.

</details>


### [65] [How Do LLMs and VLMs Understand Viewpoint Rotation Without Vision? An Interpretability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15294)
*Zhen Yang,Ping Jian,Zhongbin Guo,Zuming Zhang,Chengzhi Li,Yonghong Deng,Xinyue Zhang,Wenpeng Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了从语言视角研究空间智能的新方向——视点旋转理解(VRU)，构建了文本输入下的空间推理数据集，发现LLMs和VLMs在此任务上表现不佳，通过层间探测和因果干预揭示了模型难以绑定视点位置与对应观察的机制，并提出了选择性微调关键注意力头的方法来提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间智能研究多关注视觉空间智能，但在缺乏视觉信息时，仅凭语言智能是否足以赋予模型空间智能，以及模型如何通过纯文本输入执行相关任务仍未被探索。因此，本文从语言视角研究空间智能的基础关键能力——视点旋转理解。

Method: 1) 构建视点旋转理解(VRU)数据集，要求模型根据多步文本描述的视点旋转和观察，推断最终视点并预测对应观察；2) 对LLMs和VLMs进行层间探测分析和头间因果干预，揭示模型内部机制；3) 基于因果干预识别的关键注意力头进行选择性微调。

Result: 1) LLMs和VLMs在VRU任务上表现不佳，而人类可轻松达到100%准确率，表明当前模型能力与空间智能要求存在显著差距；2) 探测分析显示模型在隐藏状态中编码了视点信息，但在最终层难以绑定视点位置与对应观察，导致幻觉；3) 选择性微调关键注意力头可提升VRU性能，同时避免通用能力的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 本文首次从语言视角系统研究空间智能，揭示了当前模型在视点旋转理解任务上的局限性及其内在机制。提出的选择性微调方法有效提升了模型的空间推理能力，为理解和发展语言驱动的空间智能提供了新视角和方法论。

Abstract: Over the past year, spatial intelligence has drawn increasing attention. Many prior works study it from the perspective of visual-spatial intelligence, where models have access to visuospatial information from visual inputs. However, in the absence of visual information, whether linguistic intelligence alone is sufficient to endow models with spatial intelligence, and how models perform relevant tasks with text-only inputs still remain unexplored. Therefore, in this paper, we focus on a fundamental and critical capability in spatial intelligence from a linguistic perspective: viewpoint rotation understanding (VRU). Specifically, LLMs and VLMs are asked to infer their final viewpoint and predict the corresponding observation in an environment given textual description of viewpoint rotation and observation over multiple steps. We find that both LLMs and VLMs perform poorly on our proposed dataset while human can easily achieve 100% accuracy, indicating a substantial gap between current model capabilities and the requirements of spatial intelligence. To uncover the underlying mechanisms, we conduct a layer-wise probing analysis and head-wise causal intervention. Our findings reveal that although models encode viewpoint information in the hidden states, they appear to struggle to bind the viewpoint position with corresponding observation, resulting in a hallucination in final layers. Finally, we selectively fine-tune the key attention heads identified by causal intervention to improve VRU performance. Experimental results demonstrate that such selective fine-tuning achieves improved VRU performance while avoiding catastrophic forgetting of generic abilities. Our dataset and code will be released at https://github.com/Young-Zhen/VRU_Interpret .

</details>


### [66] [Diagnosing LLM Judge Reliability: Conformal Prediction Sets and Transitivity Violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15302)
*Manan Gupta,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段诊断工具包，用于评估LLM作为评判者框架在自动NLG评估中的实例级可靠性，揭示了聚合指标掩盖下的广泛不一致性，并提供了理论保证的可靠性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评判者框架在自动NLG评估中应用日益广泛，但其每个实例的可靠性仍未被充分理解。现有方法主要依赖聚合指标，可能掩盖了实例级的不一致性问题。

Method: 提出两阶段诊断工具包：1）传递性分析，检测LLM评判中的不一致性（如3-环问题）；2）使用分割保形预测集为1-5 Likert评分提供理论保证的覆盖范围，预测集宽度作为实例级可靠性指标。

Result: 研究发现：1）传递性分析显示33-67%的文档至少存在一个定向3-环，尽管聚合违规率较低（0.8-4.1%）；2）预测集宽度与可靠性显著相关（r_s=+0.576，p<10^{-100}），且在不同评判者间具有一致性（r=0.32-0.38）；3）不同评估标准可靠性差异显著，相关性最可靠（平均集大小≈3.0），连贯性中等（≈3.9），流畅性和一致性最不可靠（≈4.9）。

Conclusion: LLM作为评判者框架存在显著的实例级可靠性问题，聚合指标可能掩盖不一致性。预测集宽度可作为有效的可靠性指标，且在不同评判者间具有一致性。评估标准的可靠性差异显著，相关性最可靠，流畅性和一致性最不可靠。该诊断工具包为NLG评估提供了更精细的可靠性分析框架。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge frameworks are increasingly used for automatic NLG evaluation, yet their per-instance reliability remains poorly understood. We present a two-pronged diagnostic toolkit applied to SummEval: $\textbf{(1)}$ a transitivity analysis that reveals widespread per-input inconsistency masked by low aggregate violation rates ($\barρ = 0.8$-$4.1\%$), with $33$-$67\%$ of documents exhibiting at least one directed 3-cycle; and $\textbf{(2)}$ split conformal prediction sets over 1-5 Likert scores providing theoretically-guaranteed $\geq(1{-}α)$ coverage, with set width serving as a per-instance reliability indicator ($r_s = {+}0.576$, $N{=}1{,}918$, $p < 10^{-100}$, pooled across all judges). Critically, prediction set width shows consistent cross-judge agreement ($\bar{r} = 0.32$-$0.38$), demonstrating it captures document-level difficulty rather than judge-specific noise. Across four judges and four criteria, both diagnostics converge: criterion matters more than judge, with relevance judged most reliably (avg. set size $\approx 3.0$) and coherence moderately so (avg. set size $\approx 3.9$), while fluency and consistency remain unreliable (avg. set size $\approx 4.9$). We release all code, prompts, and cached results.

</details>


### [67] [Generalization in LLM Problem Solving: The Case of the Shortest Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15306)
*Yao Tong,Jiayuan Ye,Anastasia Borovykh,Reza Shokri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了语言模型系统泛化能力，通过最短路径规划任务发现模型在空间迁移上表现良好，但在长度扩展上因递归不稳定性而失败，分析了数据覆盖、强化学习和推理时间扩展等因素的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型能否系统泛化仍存在争议，但实证性能受训练数据、训练范式和推理策略等多因素共同影响，使得失败难以解释。作者旨在创建一个受控合成环境来分离这些因素，研究模型在不同维度上的泛化能力。

Method: 引入基于最短路径规划的受控合成环境，这是一个典型的可组合序列优化问题。该设置支持两个正交的泛化维度：空间迁移（未见地图）和长度扩展（更长视野问题）。通过分析数据覆盖、强化学习和推理时间扩展等不同学习阶段对系统问题解决的影响。

Result: 模型在空间迁移上表现良好，但在长度扩展上一致失败，主要由于递归不稳定性。数据覆盖设定了能力上限；强化学习改善了训练稳定性但未扩展这些上限；推理时间扩展提升了性能但无法挽救长度扩展失败。

Conclusion: 语言模型在受控环境中的系统泛化能力存在局限性：虽然能够进行空间迁移，但在处理更长序列问题时因递归不稳定性而失败。研究揭示了不同学习阶段对模型系统问题解决能力的影响，为理解语言模型泛化边界提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Whether language models can systematically generalize remains actively debated. Yet empirical performance is jointly shaped by multiple factors such as training data, training paradigms, and inference-time strategies, making failures difficult to interpret. We introduce a controlled synthetic environment based on shortest-path planning, a canonical composable sequential optimization problem. The setup enables clean separation of these factors and supports two orthogonal axes of generalization: spatial transfer to unseen maps and length scaling to longer-horizon problems. We find that models exhibit strong spatial transfer but consistently fail under length scaling due to recursive instability. We further analyze how distinct stages of the learning pipeline influence systematic problem-solving: for example, data coverage sets capability limits; reinforcement learning improves training stability but does not expand those limits; and inference-time scaling enhances performance but cannot rescue length-scaling failures.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [68] [QualiaNet: An Experience-Before-Inference Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14193)
*Paul Linton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出人类3D视觉的两阶段模型：经验模块提取相对注视点的立体深度，推理模块利用自然场景统计（近景产生鲜明视差梯度，远景相对平坦）来推断3D场景属性。QualiaNet计算实现该架构，通过CNN从视差图中恢复距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人类立体视觉的悖论：虽然立体视觉经验不直接提供距离信息，却影响我们对视觉尺度的推断。需要解释经验模块如何为推理模块提供信息，以及推理模块如何利用自然场景统计来估计3D属性。

Method: 提出两阶段架构：1）经验模块提取相对于注视点的立体深度（视差图）；2）推理模块利用自然场景统计（近景产生鲜明视差梯度，远景相对平坦）来估计3D场景属性。通过QualiaNet计算实现：使用模拟人类立体视觉经验的视差图，训练CNN来估计距离。

Result: QualiaNet能够仅从视差梯度中恢复距离信息，验证了所提出的两阶段架构的有效性。这表明推理模块确实可以利用视差梯度这一自然场景统计来推断3D场景属性。

Conclusion: 人类3D视觉通过经验模块提取相对深度信息，推理模块利用自然场景统计（视差梯度与距离的相关性）来推断3D场景属性。计算模型QualiaNet验证了这一机制，为理解人类立体视觉的神经机制提供了计算框架。

Abstract: Human 3D vision involves two distinct stages: an Experience Module, where stereo depth is extracted relative to fixation, and an Inference Module, where this experience is interpreted to estimate 3D scene properties. Paradoxically, although our experience of stereo vision does not provide us with distance information, it does affect our inferences about visual scale. We propose the Inference Module exploits a natural scene statistic: near scenes produce vivid disparity gradients, while far scenes appear comparatively flat. QualiaNet implements this two-stage architecture computationally: disparity maps simulating human stereo experience are passed to a CNN trained to estimate distance. The network can recover distance from disparity gradients alone, validating this approach.

</details>


### [69] [HY-World 2.0: A Multi-Modal World Model for Reconstructing, Generating, and Simulating 3D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14268)
*Team HY-World,Chenjie Cao,Xuhui Zuo,Zhenwei Wang,Yisu Zhang,Junta Wu,Zhenyang Liu,Yuning Gong,Yang Liu,Bo Yuan,Chao Zhang,Coopers Li,Dongyuan Guo,Fan Yang,Haiyu Zhang,Hang Cao,Jianchen Zhu,Jiaxin Lin,Jie Xiao,Jihong Zhang,Junlin Yu,Lei Wang,Lifu Wang,Lilin Wang,Linus,Minghui Chen,Peng He,Penghao Zhao,Qi Chen,Rui Chen,Rui Shao,Sicong Liu,Wangchen Qin,Xiaochuan Niu,Xiang Yuan,Yi Sun,Yifei Tang,Yifu Sun,Yihang Lian,Yonghao Tan,Yuhong Liu,Yuyang Yin,Zhiyuan Min,Tengfei Wang,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HY-World 2.0是一个多模态世界模型框架，能够从文本、单视图图像、多视图图像和视频生成3D世界表示，通过四阶段方法合成可导航的3D高斯溅射场景，并提供了高性能的渲染平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在HY-World 1.0基础上，开发更先进的多模态3D世界生成框架，支持多样化的输入模态，生成高质量、可导航的3D场景，并在开源方法中达到最先进性能。

Method: 采用四阶段方法：1) HY-Pano 2.0全景图生成；2) WorldNav轨迹规划；3) WorldStereo 2.0世界扩展（基于关键帧的视图生成模型）；4) WorldMirror 2.0世界组合（前馈模型用于通用3D预测）。同时开发了WorldLens高性能3DGS渲染平台。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，HY-World 2.0在开源方法中达到了最先进的性能，结果与闭源模型Marble相当。模型权重、代码和技术细节已全部公开。

Conclusion: HY-World 2.0成功构建了一个强大的多模态3D世界生成框架，通过创新的四阶段方法和渲染平台，实现了从多种输入生成高质量3D场景的能力，为3D世界模型研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: We introduce HY-World 2.0, a multi-modal world model framework that advances our prior project HY-World 1.0. HY-World 2.0 accommodates diverse input modalities, including text prompts, single-view images, multi-view images, and videos, and produces 3D world representations. With text or single-view image inputs, the model performs world generation, synthesizing high-fidelity, navigable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scenes. This is achieved through a four-stage method: a) Panorama Generation with HY-Pano 2.0, b) Trajectory Planning with WorldNav, c) World Expansion with WorldStereo 2.0, and d) World Composition with WorldMirror 2.0. Specifically, we introduce key innovations to enhance panorama fidelity, enable 3D scene understanding and planning, and upgrade WorldStereo, our keyframe-based view generation model with consistent memory. We also upgrade WorldMirror, a feed-forward model for universal 3D prediction, by refining model architecture and learning strategy, enabling world reconstruction from multi-view images or videos. Also, we introduce WorldLens, a high-performance 3DGS rendering platform featuring a flexible engine-agnostic architecture, automatic IBL lighting, efficient collision detection, and training-rendering co-design, enabling interactive exploration of 3D worlds with character support. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HY-World 2.0 achieves state-of-the-art performance on several benchmarks among open-source approaches, delivering results comparable to the closed-source model Marble. We release all model weights, code, and technical details to facilitate reproducibility and support further research on 3D world models.

</details>


### [70] [Geometrically Consistent Multi-View Scene Generation from Freehand Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14302)
*Ahmed Bourouis,Savas Ozkan,Andrea Maracani,Yi-Zhe Song,Mete Ozay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 从单张手绘草图生成几何一致的多视角场景，通过构建数据集、几何注意力适配器和稀疏对应监督损失解决训练数据缺失、几何推理和跨视角一致性三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手绘草图是几何信息最贫乏的多视角生成输入，通过抽象笔画传达场景意图但包含空间扭曲，与一致3D解释相冲突。现有方法需要照片、文本或多视角输入，而草图到3D方法需要多视角或昂贵的逐场景优化。

Method: 提出三个相互增强的贡献：(1) 通过自动生成和过滤管道构建约9k个草图到多视角样本的数据集；(2) 平行相机感知注意力适配器(CA3)，将几何归纳偏置注入视频变换器；(3) 从运动恢复结构重建中推导的稀疏对应监督损失(CSL)。框架在单个去噪过程中合成所有视角，无需参考图像、迭代细化或逐场景优化。

Result: 方法显著优于最先进的两阶段基线，真实感(FID)提升超过60%，几何一致性(Corr-Acc)提升23%，同时提供高达3.7倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: 成功解决了从单张手绘草图生成几何一致多视角场景的新问题，通过数据集构建、几何注意力机制和对应监督损失的综合方法，实现了高质量、高效率的多视角生成。

Abstract: We tackle a new problem: generating geometrically consistent multi-view scenes from a single freehand sketch. Freehand sketches are the most geometrically impoverished input one could offer a multi-view generator. They convey scene intent through abstract strokes while introducing spatial distortions that actively conflict with any consistent 3D interpretation. No prior method attempts this; existing multi-view approaches require photographs or text, while sketch-to-3D methods need multiple views or costly per-scene optimisation.
  We address three compounding challenges; absent training data, the need for geometric reasoning from distorted 2D input, and cross-view consistency, through three mutually reinforcing contributions: (i) a curated dataset of $\sim$9k sketch-to-multiview samples, constructed via an automated generation and filtering pipeline; (ii) Parallel Camera-Aware Attention Adapters (CA3) that inject geometric inductive biases into the video transformer; and (iii) a Sparse Correspondence Supervision Loss (CSL) derived from Structure-from-Motion reconstructions.
  Our framework synthesizes all views in a single denoising process without requiring reference images, iterative refinement, or per-scene optimization. Our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art two-stage baselines, improving realism (FID) by over 60% and geometric consistency (Corr-Acc) by 23%, while providing up to a 3.7$\times$ inference speedup.

</details>


### [71] [Interpretable Human Activity Recognition for Subtle Robbery Detection in Surveillance Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14329)
*Bryan Jhoan Cazáres Leyva,Ulises Gachuz Davila,José Juan González Fonseca,Juan Irving Vasquez,Vanessa A. Camacho-Vázquez,Sergio Isahí Garrido-Castañeda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于姿态的混合方法，用于实时检测非暴力街头抢劫（抢夺逃跑），结合实时感知与可解释分类，适合边缘设备部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非暴力街头抢劫（抢夺逃跑）难以自动检测，因为其短暂、细微，在无约束监控视频中与良性人类互动难以区分。需要一种能够在边缘设备上实时运行且具有良好泛化能力的检测方法。

Method: 采用混合姿态驱动方法：1) 使用YOLO姿态估计器提取跟踪人物的身体关键点；2) 计算运动学和交互特征（手部速度、手臂伸展、接近度、攻击者-受害者相对运动）；3) 基于这些特征训练随机森林分类器；4) 应用时间滞后滤波器稳定帧级预测并减少误报。

Result: 在模拟数据集和互联网视频独立测试集上评估，展示了在不同场景和摄像机视角下的良好泛化能力。在NVIDIA Jetson Nano上实现完整流水线，报告了实时性能，支持主动式设备端抢劫检测的可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效检测非暴力街头抢劫事件，具有实时性能、良好泛化能力和边缘部署可行性，为主动式设备端监控系统提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Non-violent street robberies (snatch-and-run) are difficult to detect automatically because they are brief, subtle, and often indistinguishable from benign human interactions in unconstrained surveillance footage. This paper presents a hybrid, pose-driven approach for detecting snatch-and-run events that combines real-time perception with an interpretable classification stage suitable for edge deployment. The system uses a YOLO-based pose estimator to extract body keypoints for each tracked person and computes kinematic and interaction features describing hand speed, arm extension, proximity, and relative motion between an aggressor-victim pair. A Random Forest classifier is trained on these descriptors, and a temporal hysteresis filter is applied to stabilize frame-level predictions and reduce spurious alarms. We evaluate the method on a staged dataset and on a disjoint test set collected from internet videos, demonstrating promising generalization across different scenes and camera viewpoints. Finally, we implement the complete pipeline on an NVIDIA Jetson Nano and report real-time performance, supporting the feasibility of proactive, on-device robbery detection.

</details>


### [72] [SatBLIP: Context Understanding and Feature Identification from Satellite Imagery with Vision-Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14373)
*Xue Wu,Shengting Cao,Jiaqi Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SatBLIP：基于卫星视觉语言框架的农村环境风险理解与特征识别方法，通过对比学习与引导式字幕生成预测县级社会脆弱性指数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农村环境风险受地方特定条件（如住房质量、道路通达性、地表模式）影响，但现有脆弱性指数过于粗糙，无法深入理解风险背景。传统遥感方法存在手工特征提取、人工虚拟审核和自然图像训练的视觉语言模型等局限性。

Method: 提出SatBLIP框架：1）使用GPT-4o生成卫星图块的结构化描述（屋顶类型/状况、房屋大小、庭院属性、绿化、道路环境）；2）微调卫星适应的BLIP模型为未见图像生成字幕；3）通过CLIP编码字幕，与LLM生成的嵌入通过注意力机制融合；4）在空间聚合下进行SVI估计；5）使用SHAP识别关键特征。

Result: 识别出驱动稳健预测的关键属性：屋顶形式/状况、街道宽度、植被、汽车/开放空间等，实现了农村风险环境的可解释性映射。

Conclusion: SatBLIP通过结合对比图像-文本对齐与针对卫星语义的引导式字幕生成，克服了传统遥感方法的局限性，为农村环境风险提供了细粒度、可解释的理解框架。

Abstract: Rural environmental risks are shaped by place-based conditions (e.g., housing quality, road access, land-surface patterns), yet standard vulnerability indices are coarse and provide limited insight into risk contexts. We propose SatBLIP, a satellite-specific vision-language framework for rural context understanding and feature identification that predicts county-level Social Vulnerability Index (SVI). SatBLIP addresses limitations of prior remote sensing pipelines-handcrafted features, manual virtual audits, and natural-image-trained VLMs-by coupling contrastive image-text alignment with bootstrapped captioning tailored to satellite semantics. We use GPT-4o to generate structured descriptions of satellite tiles (roof type/condition, house size, yard attributes, greenery, and road context), then fine-tune a satellite-adapted BLIP model to generate captions for unseen images. Captions are encoded with CLIP and fused with LLM-derived embeddings via attention for SVI estimation under spatial aggregation. Using SHAP, we identify salient attributes (e.g., roof form/condition, street width, vegetation, cars/open space) that consistently drive robust predictions, enabling interpretable mapping of rural risk environments.

</details>


### [73] [FoodSense: A Multisensory Food Dataset and Benchmark for Predicting Taste, Smell, Texture, and Sound from Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14388)
*Sabab Ishraq,Aarushi Aarushi,Juncai Jiang,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FoodSense：首个用于从食物图像预测多感官体验（味觉、嗅觉、触觉、听觉）的数据集和基准模型，包含66,842个人类标注的图像-参与者配对，并生成基于图像的推理轨迹来训练能同时预测评分和提供解释的视觉语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言研究主要关注食物识别（如餐食识别、成分检测、营养估计），而人类能从食物图像中推断多感官体验（味觉、嗅觉、纹理、声音）这一认知科学现象尚未在计算机视觉中得到充分探索。需要填补图像到多感官预测的研究空白。

Method: 1) 创建FoodSense数据集：包含66,842个参与者-图像配对，覆盖2,987个独特食物图像，每个配对包含四个感官维度（味觉、嗅觉、纹理、声音）的1-5分评分和自由文本描述；2) 扩展标注：使用大语言模型基于图像、评分和描述生成视觉依据的推理轨迹；3) 训练FoodSense-VL基准模型：基于这些标注训练视觉语言模型，直接从食物图像生成多感官评分和基于图像的合理解释。

Result: 建立了首个用于跨感官推理的人类标注数据集，开发了能同时预测多感官评分和提供视觉依据解释的基准模型，展示了现有流行评估指标在视觉感官推理任务上的不足，连接了认知科学中的跨感官感知与现代多模态模型指令调优。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了从食物图像预测多感官体验的研究空白，通过FoodSense数据集和基准模型为视觉感官推理建立了新基准，揭示了当前评估指标的局限性，为连接认知科学发现与多模态模型发展提供了重要桥梁。

Abstract: Humans routinely infer taste, smell, texture, and even sound from food images a phenomenon well studied in cognitive science. However, prior vision language research on food has focused primarily on recognition tasks such as meal identification, ingredient detection, and nutrition estimation. Image-based prediction of multisensory experience remains largely unexplored. We introduce FoodSense, a human-annotated dataset for cross-sensory inference containing 66,842 participant-image pairs across 2,987 unique food images. Each pair includes numeric ratings (1-5) and free-text descriptors for four sensory dimensions: taste, smell, texture, and sound. To enable models to both predict and explain sensory expectations, we expand short human annotations into image-grounded reasoning traces. A large language model generates visual justifications conditioned on the image, ratings, and descriptors. Using these annotations, we train FoodSense-VL, a vision language benchmark model to produce both multisensory ratings and grounded explanations directly from food images. This work connects cognitive science findings on cross-sensory perception with modern instruction tuning for multimodal models and shows that many popular evaluation metrics are insufficient for visually sensory inference.

</details>


### [74] [Zero-Ablation Overstates Register Content Dependence in DINO Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14433)
*Felipe Parodi,Jordan Matelsky,Melanie Segado*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 零消融（zero-ablation）在视觉Transformer中高估了对寄存器（registers）精确内容的依赖，而替换控制方法（均值替换、噪声替换、跨图像寄存器混洗）能保持性能，表明任务只需要合理的寄存器类激活而非精确的图像特定值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究零消融在视觉Transformer中评估寄存器功能时的有效性，因为零消融显示寄存器对性能至关重要，但可能过度强调了对其精确内容的依赖。

Method: 使用三种替换控制方法：均值替换（用均值向量替换激活）、噪声替换（用高斯噪声替换）、跨图像寄存器混洗（在不同图像间交换寄存器激活），并与零消融对比。通过分类、对应性、分割任务评估，并分析每块余弦相似度来衡量内部表示的扰动程度。

Result: 零消融导致性能大幅下降（分类下降36.6pp，分割下降30.9pp），但三种替换控制方法在各项任务中均保持性能，与未修改基线相差约1pp以内。余弦相似度分析显示替换方法确实扰动了内部表示，但零消融造成不成比例的大扰动。结果在ViT-B规模上可复现。

Conclusion: 零消融夸大了对寄存器精确内容的依赖。在冻结特征评估中，性能依赖于合理的寄存器类激活，而非精确的图像特定值。寄存器仍起到缓冲密集特征免受[CLS]依赖影响的作用，并与压缩的块几何结构相关。

Abstract: Zero-ablation -- replacing token activations with zero vectors -- is widely used to probe token function in vision transformers. Register zeroing in DINOv2+registers and DINOv3 produces large drops (up to $-36.6$\,pp classification, $-30.9$\,pp segmentation), suggesting registers are functionally indispensable. However, three replacement controls -- mean-substitution, noise-substitution, and cross-image register-shuffling -- preserve performance across classification, correspondence, and segmentation, remaining within ${\sim}1$\,pp of the unmodified baseline. Per-patch cosine similarity shows these replacements genuinely perturb internal representations, while zeroing causes disproportionately large perturbations, consistent with why it alone degrades tasks. We conclude that zero-ablation overstates dependence on exact register content. In the frozen-feature evaluations we test, performance depends on plausible register-like activations rather than on exact image-specific values. Registers nevertheless buffer dense features from \texttt{[CLS]} dependence and are associated with compressed patch geometry. These findings, including the replacement-control results, replicate at ViT-B scale.

</details>


### [75] [Crowdsourcing of Real-world Image Annotation via Visual Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14449)
*Xiaolei Diao,Fausto Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合知识表示、自然语言处理和计算机视觉的图像标注方法，通过视觉属性约束减少标注者主观性，并引入基于对象类别层次结构和标注者反馈的交互式众包框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据为中心的人工智能在对象识别数据集中存在局限性，主要问题源于语义鸿沟问题，导致视觉数据与语言描述之间存在复杂的多对多映射，这种偏差对计算机视觉任务性能产生负面影响

Method: 提出图像标注方法，整合知识表示、自然语言处理和计算机视觉技术，通过应用视觉属性约束减少标注者主观性；引入交互式众包框架，基于预定义的对象类别层次结构和标注者反馈动态提问，通过视觉属性指导图像标注

Result: 实验证明了该方法的有效性，并讨论了标注者反馈以优化众包设置

Conclusion: 该方法通过整合多模态技术和交互式众包框架，有效减少了图像标注中的主观性和语义鸿沟问题，提高了标注质量和计算机视觉任务性能
